| R124 | **The owner's form rules in the product: the shared form parts and the reminder form (forms PR 1 of 3; the mockups of 2026-10-23, `scoring/p1-rig` 935214a, dbe1ab4, 9e95c9d, ported without their switch).** **The owner's rules, verbatim as agreed:** C1 "one column grid"; C2 "height by function: 48 dp primary, 42 dp secondary, 38 dp tertiary; fields share one height; no control is taller than the field beside it"; C3 "one field pattern: the title always small inside the frame; nothing inside a box runs past one line; help or error one line below"; C4 "consistency across screens"; C5 "Salva e Annulla are always the same length, no matter their height, and they sit on the right, with Salva aligned to the right, Annulla to its left, at least 14 dp distance. They are also always two different colors, Annulla being more desaturated… always in the same place, floating above the nav bar at the bottom", separated from the content by a tonal surface, not a line, and a sheet carries the same bar as its anchored foot, grows upward only and never moves the control just tapped; C6 "weekdays and month days are 42 dp text toggles on a 7-column grid spanning the form's edges, one component". Help or error starts at the field's text inset (16 dp); rules show only when broken (example help such as "es. 50 mg" stays); the space for an error is reserved under every field that can show one ("Reserve it"), so nothing moves; Avanzate is grouped rows, always showing, in the dependency order **Quando prendo, Notifica, Se non rispondo, Durata**. **Why scoring missed it:** P2 ranked each variant against the others on one base screen, so a defect every variant shared (a Salva that scrolled away, two field patterns, chips of three heights, a weekday picker of checkboxes) cancelled out of every comparison and no ranking could see it. The rig therefore added absolute checks with fixed thresholds, run before any ranking: C1–C6 (grid, height by function, field pattern, consistency, the Salva/Annulla bar, the day picker), then C7–C10 (help start, grid reach, help is not a rule, disabled looks disabled) when C1's right-edge rule turned out to exempt every chip and day row and so could never catch a short grid. **Built:** `ui/form/` holds the parts every form will share: `FormTextField` (Material 3 1.4's state-based `OutlinedTextField` with `TextFieldLabelPosition.Attached(alwaysMinimize = true)`, one line, help or error as one reserved line), `FormTimeField` and `FormDateField` (writable, a picker icon in the frame; "Scrivila come hh:mm" / "Data non valida" keep Salva off), `FieldPair`, `FieldHelpLine`, `FormFootBar` (Salva 48 dp filled, Annulla 42 dp tonal, equal widths, 16 dp apart, on `surfaceContainer`), `FormScaffold` (content scrolls, the foot stays above the navigation bar), `FormSheetScaffold` (the same foot on `surfaceContainerHigh`; Annulla, Back and the scrim ask when edited), `SpanGrid`, `GridToggleCell`, `DayGrid`/`WeekdayGrid`, `FormGroupRow` and `FormSwitchRow` (one-line help). The reminder form (add and edit, all four dose types and the two alert forms) is built on them: "Ora", "Dal", "Fino al" and the window’s ends are writable fields; the repeat kinds (Daily, Specific days, Every N days, Active/pause cycle, as on master) are 42 dp cells on the grid, which will take Giorni del mese and A-10's combination in PR 3; the weekdays are the 42 dp `WeekdayGrid`; the interval is "Ogni (ore)" in the field pattern; Avanzate's four rows say their values in one line and each opens `ReminderGroupSheet` with master's settings grouped as in the rig (Quando prendo: ask the amount, instructions; Notifica: weekend mode, auto-confirm; Se non rispondo: repeat every/max, stay until answered, heads-up minutes; Durata: Dal/Fino al, a set number of doses), each sheet editing a draft its own Salva applies. Dependent fields stay in place, disabled while their switch is off. **Fixes:** "repeat every 0 min" saved silently (the snapshot stored 0): `AdvancedReminderFieldsState.errors()` now refuses a value below 1 for every-min, max repeats, heads-up minutes and doses, and an end before the start; the field says "Almeno 1 minuto", the sheet's Salva is off, Back asks, the stored value is unchanged, and the form's Salva is also off while any such value stands. The cycle's pause days said "Almeno 1" for a check that refused only negatives; the scheduler accepts 0 (`isActiveOn` requires `pauseDays >= 0`, `matchingDateOnOrAfter` guards only a negative), so the check stays `>= 0` and the message is now "0 o più" / "0 or more" (also on the alert forms' threshold and lead days). **Strings, EN / IT (old → new):** `add_reminder_instructions` "Instructions (optional)" → "Instructions" / "Istruzioni (facoltative)" → "Istruzioni"; `add_reminder_every_hours` "Every ___ hours" → "Every (hours)" / "Ogni ___ ore" → "Ogni (ore)"; the switch help lines, one line each: `add_reminder_sticky_desc` → "Can’t be swiped away, even when locked" / "Non si può scorrere via, nemmeno da bloccato", `add_reminder_pre_alarm_desc` → "A silent notice a few minutes ahead" / "Un avviso silenzioso qualche minuto prima", `add_reminder_variable_amount_desc` → "Take opens a field with the dose to change" / "Prendi apre un campo con la dose, modificabile", `add_reminder_course_desc` → "Stops on its own after the last dose" / "Si ferma da solo dopo l’ultima dose", `add_reminder_push_this_reminder_to_the_end_of_the_weekend` → "Weekends: moved to the window’s end (Settings)" / "Nel weekend va a fine finestra (Impostazioni)", `add_reminder_silently_mark_taken_no_notification_for` → "Marked taken, no notification (for others)" / "Segnata presa, senza notifica (per altri)"; renamed with new text: `add_reminder_minutes_after_parent_1_1440` "Minutes after parent (1–1440)" → `add_reminder_minutes_after` "Minutes after" / "Minuti dopo", its rule `add_reminder_minutes_after_range` "1 to 1440" / "Da 1 a 1440" shown only when broken; `add_reminder_cycle_starts_today_the_anchor_date_isn_t` → `add_reminder_cycle_help` "The cycle starts today" / "Il ciclo inizia oggi" (shown only while the anchor is today); removed: `add_reminder_active_date_range`, `add_reminder_leave_unset_for_no_bound_a_taper_is_just`; `add_reminder_instructions_hint` moved from placeholder to the field's help line; 28 new keys (`form_*`, `reminder_group_*`, `reminder_sum_*`, `reminder_range_help`). | ✅ **Resolved.** **The rules as gates:** `ReminderFormRulesTest` runs C1–C10 (ported from the rig's `FormAuditBase` into `ui/form/FormRules`, `FormProbe`, `FormScreenTestBase`: the real `EmivitaNavHost`, it-IT, 411 × 891 dp, native graphics) on 10 surfaces at font 1.0 and 2.0 (the form at rest, weekdays, the four sheets, interval, window, follow-up, edit) and C4 across the form and its four sheets: 21 tests, every check passes. They fail when a rule breaks: Salva and Annulla swapped, `C5 Salva/Annulla: 2 violation(s): Salva ends 112.0 dp from the right edge; Annulla is not to the left of Salva on its line`; a two-line placeholder, `C3 one field pattern: 1 violation(s): 'Istruzioni\nIstruzioni' wraps to 2 lines inside the box`. **Behaviour:** `ReminderFormBehaviourTest` (7: every 0 min refused and never stored, the foot and the tapped switch unmoved; a typed time read and "25:00" refused; typed dates read, 31/02 and an end before the start refused; a sheet applies only on its Salva; Back from an edited form asks; the Avanzate rows say the stored values; 0 pause days accepted, −1 refused), `FormFieldParsingTest` (4). Fail-before: with the 0-minute check removed, `nothing saved silently: the default stays expected:<30> but was:<0>`. Existing reminder tests unchanged and green: `FormRotationTest`, `RepeatUntilAnsweredDefaultTest`, `WindowEndsNextDayNoteTest`. Gate (1 worker, low priority, on master at 5319bb5): `assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug` **666 tests, 0 failures, 1 skipped** (130 result files, cleared first), lint clean (hints only: seven GradleDependency, one NewerVersionAvailable, two baseline notes; lint's ModifierParameter caught the field parts' first default modifier, fixed, and the four AutoboxingStateCreation hints in the alert forms are gone); `purpose_map --check` (`sheet:ReminderGroupSheet` placed under the reminder form), `code_health --selftest` (14) and `--check` (0 mechanical findings), `strings_review --check` (0 findings), `palette_derive --check` and `splash_icon --check` PASS; `:app:assembleRelease` and `r8_check` PASS (2 widgets survived R8). **Not done here (PR 2):** the medicine form (add and edit), the quick-add sheet, Stock & expiry, Medical ID, Alarms & snooze's snooze length and Weekend mode (weekday chips → `WeekdayGrid`) onto the foot bar and the field pattern; the dialogs (`DoseEditDialog`, `ColorPickerDialog`, the journal note, the backup password) keep their dialog buttons until the owner says whether C5 applies to a dialog. **PR 3:** Giorni del mese and A-10's combination on the grid, interval units and instruction samples, B-38's importance picker in the Notifica sheet. | R126 | **The owner's answers of 2026-10-24 to R125's questions, and one phone finding (forms PR 2 of 3, PR #50).** **The owner's answers, verbatim:** (1) "Grow as you type"; (2) "Shorter labels, side by side"; (3) "Titles"; (4) "Ask when edited". **(1) Grow as you type.** `FormTextField(freeText = true)` replaces R125's `maxLines`: empty it is one line, the height of every other field (56 dp at font 1.0, measured), it grows a line at a time as it is typed up to `FreeTextMaxLines` (6), then scrolls inside; its title, help and error stay one line. Free text: Medical ID's allergies and the doctor's address and the Dettagli sheet's notes (the three fields R125 made single-line; before R125 the address and allergies were `singleLine = false` and the notes 2 to 6 lines), the journal note's edit dialog (was `maxLines = 6`) and the dose edit's note (was `maxLines = 4`, now 6, the same as the others); the journal's draft field on the detail page keeps its own field and placeholder, its limit raised from 3 lines to the same 6. C3 in `FormRules`: a field is declared free text in the test's `FormDecl(freeText = setOf(label prefix))`, the counterpart of the code's `freeText = true`; for such a field only the typed value may run to several lines (C3 no longer reports "the value wraps"), and once it does it is left out of C2's and C4's shared field height; empty or on one line it is held to that height. Its title, a placeholder and its help still fail on a second line. **(2) Shorter labels, side by side.** EN / IT (old → new): `unsaved_keep` "Keep editing" → "Edit" / "Continua a modificare" → "Modifica" (`unsaved_discard` "Discard" / "Scarta" unchanged); `recovery_restore` "Restore from backup" → "Restore" / "Ripristina da backup" → "Ripristina"; `recovery_continue_empty` "Continue empty" → "Without data" / "Continua senza dati" → "Senza dati". Both dialogs' pairs are side by side; nothing else stacked, so `ActionPairLayout`'s stacked path (`stackWhenCut`) is removed. The question before edits are dropped is one composable, `UnsavedQuestion`, asked by `unsavedChangesGuard`, `FormSheetScaffold` and the Settings sheets. **(3) Titles.** Stock & expiry keeps its titled groups, as built. **(4) Ask when edited.** Alarms & snooze and Weekend mode report whether their form is edited (`rememberDirty`: the snooze length; Weekend's switch, days and window, against the stored values once read) to `SettingsScreen`, which owns their sheet: Annulla, the back arrow, Back and the scrim ask when edited and leave at once when not, as `FormSheetScaffold` does (Back and the scrim hide the sheet first; "Modifica" shows it again with the edits). | R140 | **W4 of the R139 walk: a due dose vanished from Today while OxygenOS held its alarm (9 Pro, 2026-11-01, 00:05 to 00:11).** Both causes R139 left unseparated are real mechanisms, and each now has a test that failed before the fix. (b) The re-projection: every pass of `OccurrenceProjectionCache` walked from now, so a slot past its time whose alarm the phone was still holding (armed, not fired, no dose row) dropped out of the projection, and Today draws only the projection and the dose rows. Any pass after 00:05 hid the dose until the alarm fired: returning to Today registers its window, and every write invalidates. `TodayDeferredAlarmTest` renders Today on the real container with the slot three minutes back and its alarm armed: before the fix, no row (the assertion failed); its control, a reminder made after its time, which arms tomorrow, so today's slot must not come back, passed before and after. Fix: the cache walks each reminder from its armed slot when that slot is past (`armedSlots`, read from `AlarmWatchdog.plannedSlots`, the record `AlarmScheduler.schedule` already keeps for the missed-alarm notice), so the held dose is projected, counted against the stock, and filtered by the answered set as before. Today's progress line counts a projected slot at or before now as due ("1 da prendere ora", not "prossima alle 00:05"), and so do the widget's count and the Medicines card (`OccurrenceProjectionCacheTest`, `TodayProgressTest`, `MedicineListStateTest`). (a) The day: `rememberCurrentDate` waited for midnight in one `delay`, which counts on the monotonic clock, and that clock stops in deep sleep, so a wait computed at 23:50 could still be running at 00:10. `CurrentDateTest` moves the wall clock 20 minutes while the monotonic one stands still: before, the date was still 31 Oct 61 s later; now the wall clock is re-read at least once a minute, and the awake control turns at midnight both before and after. Which one the 9 Pro hit is not established on the device: after the alarm fired Today showed the dose under 1 Nov, which needs the day to have turned, so (b) is necessary to explain what was seen and (a) may have added to it. No night walk was run to separate them. Unchanged, an owner decision: the row of a held dose still looks like an upcoming one (muted, with Prendi ora). Found by reading, not tested or fixed: `rescheduleOnLaunch` re-arms every reminder from now, so opening the app in the first minute after a slot the OS is holding moves that alarm to tomorrow, and `MissedDoseBackfiller`'s one-minute grace leaves the slot out too, so that dose gets no row at all. **Gate**: (test results cleared first) `assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug`, exit 0: 859 tests, 848 passed, 11 skipped (the 384 dp measuring twins), 0 failed; lint no errors or warnings beyond the baseline; code health and purpose map pass. `assembleRelease` and `r8_check.py` left to CI. | ✅ Resolved | **R143** — its walk from an armed past slot took a slot that had rung for one still held (an interval counted from the answer keeps its record there); only an armed slot with no dose row comes back |
| R141 | **Opening the app in the minute after a slot the OS is holding lost the dose (found by reading while fixing R140, 2026-11-01).** `rescheduleOnLaunch` re-arms every reminder from now, and then `MissedDoseBackfiller` writes the slots that passed, but it leaves the last minute (`GRACE_MILLIS`) to the alarm path, which may be firing them. A slot inside that minute whose alarm the OS was still holding, as OxygenOS held W4's, lost both: the re-arm replaced its alarm with tomorrow's, the backfill skipped the slot, and the dose got no row and no notification. `LaunchHeldSlotTest` runs the launch path's two steps, in its order, on the real container, with the slot a few seconds back and its alarm armed: before the fix, no alarm was left at the slot (the assertion failed). Its control is a slot that already fired: the launch must not arm it again, since that would ring a second time for a dose already posted. It passed before and after. Fix: `AlarmWatchdog.heldSlots` lists the armed slots inside that last minute with no alarm fired since, and `RealSchedulingCoordinator.rescheduleAll` arms such a slot again as it is instead of moving it, so AlarmManager delivers it at once and the receiver materialises, posts and re-arms the next slot as usual (`AlarmWatchdogTest`). The two rules now agree: the last minute belongs to the alarm path, in the backfill and in the re-arm. A slot held longer than a minute was already covered: the backfill writes it as a pending row and `UnansweredDoseReposter` posts it again. **Gate**: (test results cleared first, on the tree merged with R139) `assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug`, exit 0: 862 tests, 851 passed, 11 skipped (the 384 dp measuring twins), 0 failed; lint has hints only; code health and purpose map pass. `assembleRelease` and `r8_check.py` left to CI. | ✅ Resolved | **R143** — the one-minute window and the any-alarm guard are replaced by one rule in `scheduleOrCancel`; the back-fill did not cover a longer hold when the app had been seen after the slot |

| R143 | **The gaps a fresh-eyes review of PR #62 found before merge, closed: a dose no alarm would post read as due, and a re-arm while the OS held a dose replaced it (2026-11-02).** (C1) An interval counted from the answer re-arms only once its dose is answered, so its alarm record stays on the slot that rang. R140's pass walked on from that record, taking the open dose as answered there, and R142's rule made every interval since one more dose due, with Take and Skip, in the line, the rows and the Medicines card; a pass from before such a slot did the same at the minute's tick. Taking one wrote a dose never scheduled and took it from the stock. (I1) Take or Skip on a held dose re-armed its reminder from now, which replaced the held alarm, and a "when reminded" follow-up is armed only by that alarm ringing: none was. (I2, older than this branch) An edit of the reminder, or an answer to another of its doses, re-armed it from now and lost the held dose: no row, no notification. (I3) R141 kept only a slot of the last minute, and its claim that the back-fill covers a longer hold was false whenever the app had been seen after the slot: an opening in its minute, then another after it, lost the dose; and its guard read any alarm of that minute as this one's (M4). Fix: one rule, `AlarmWatchdog.heldSlots`, read by the projection and the re-arm alike: an armed slot already past, after the last restart (BootRescheduleReceiver stamps it), within the re-post's day, whose dose has no row yet, since a slot that rang has its row. The projection brings back that slot alone and marks an occurrence `armed` when its alarm is armed for it (never for a reminder that answers itself, M7); Today, its line, the widget's count and the Medicines card turn only an armed slot due. `scheduleOrCancel`, which every re-arm passes through, arms a held slot again as it is, so it is delivered at once and its alarm does the rest; that replaces R141's launch-only window. A Take or Skip that writes a row ahead of its alarm runs the "when reminded" cascade (`onDoseMaterialized`, the receiver's cascade moved into the coordinator). Also: the line reads the rows' clock, and Today's clock can be set (`rememberNowState`), so R142's "turns due while Today is open" is now a test; the Medicines card follows the minute's clock (M3); a reminder slot's row keeps its key when its alarm writes the row (M1); two comments that called projected rows not actionable are corrected (M5); the launch test waits a second at most, and the held Today tests no longer skip after midnight (M6). History's day cell still counts rows only (M8, by design). Tests failing before the fix: `TodayDeferredAlarmTest` (an unanswered "when taken" dose is due once, not at each interval since; a slot still ahead at the last pass does not turn due; a held slot of a reminder that answers itself does not read due), `LaunchHeldSlotTest` (a slot held past its minute; another reminder's alarm during the hold), the new `HeldSlotRearmTest` (an edit; another dose answered; Take arms the follow-up) and `MedicineListStateTest`. The first red run started two launch tests at a minute's turn, outside R141's window, so the helper waits a second there and a second red run gave clean failures. Controls passing before and after: a "when taken" slot that rang is not armed again, a slot from before a restart is left to the back-fill, an armed slot turns due while Today is open. Not covered: the Grouped, Compact and Hero layouts with a held dose (they read the same status), and a phone walk. **Gate**: with test results cleared first, `assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug` exited 0: 878 tests (14 new), 867 passed, 11 skipped, 0 failed; lint shows hints only. `code_health.py --check` and `purpose_map.py --check` pass. `assembleRelease` and `r8_check.py` are left to CI. | ✅ Resolved |  |

| R145 | **What R144's walk left found but not fixed, and what a fresh review of PR #65 found beside it: an archive left its cards, no path cancelled a gone dose's repeats, and the summary still lagged the shade (2026-11-02).** The owner asked for these and any other gaps closed before the merge. A review agent read the PR's diff and its callers, found no defect in it, and found three gaps beside it; each was checked against the code before fixing. (A1) Archiving (Elimina) dismissed nothing: the medicine left Today and its reminders stopped, but its due cards stayed in the shade, answerable. `archive` now takes its pending doses' cards away, reading them once the alarms are off, so a dose that fires during the archive goes too (the review's third point). The doses stay PENDING in the record. (A2) No path cancelled a gone dose's nag or snooze: an archive, a delete for good and a reminder's delete left them armed, to fire into a receiver that finds no reminder (seen twice on the walk). The shared dismisser now cancels both with the card (`AlarmScheduler.cancelRefires`; a PendingIntent matches without its extras) and forgets the snooze ledger's entry; on an answer this is what `resolve` already did for the nag. (A3, the review's first point) A snooze from the picker or the alarm screen, and a move or a soft delete from the dose's dialog, took the card away with `ReminderNotifications.dismiss` alone, so the summary kept counting it and its Take all still took the snoozed or moved dose. `dismiss` now refreshes the summary itself, and the three callers that refreshed after it no longer do. (A4, the review's second point) R144 left out only the ids of the refresh at hand, but Take all answers one dose at a time, each with its own refresh, while the shade may still list the earlier ones: the summary could say "1 dose" over none. The builder now remembers for ten seconds the cards it was told were going, and posting a card again (an undo) takes it off that list. Kept, an owner rule: a follow-up still rings after its parent is skipped, since medTimer anchors a linked reminder on the source's processed time, which a skip sets as a take does (Appendix E A-22, C-05). Tests, each failing before its fix: `NotificationCarryThroughTest` (an archive takes the card and keeps the dose; an archive, a delete for good and a reminder delete each take the dose's nag; a card taken away directly leaves the summary counting one, with no Take all) and `ReminderNotificationPolishTest` (two refreshes, one dose each, leave no summary). Walked on the 9 Pro (alarm stream at its minimum, put back to 11): a dose posted at 18:51 with its 19:21 repeat armed; the archive took the card, the repeat and tomorrow's alarm; the medicine was then deleted for good. **Gate**: with test results cleared first, `assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug` exited 0: 887 tests (6 new), 876 passed, 11 skipped, 0 failed; lint shows no errors or warnings. `code_health.py --check` and `purpose_map.py --check` pass. `assembleRelease` and `r8_check.py` are left to CI. | ✅ Resolved |  |
| R146 | **master's CI failed on #65's merge commit (e50d064) in a test whose tree had passed on the PR head: an `eventually` block that could not wait (2026-11-02).** `TodayDeferredAlarmTest` ("a held slot's row is a due one, and Skip answers that slot before its alarm arrives") failed with `NoSuchElementException: List is empty` at its `h.doseEvents(reminderId).single()`, inside `eventually`. This file's `eventually` retried only on `AssertionError`; `.single()` on an empty list throws something else, so when the Skip's write (Dispatchers.IO) had not landed by the first check the test ended there instead of waiting. On the PR run the write won the race; on master it lost. The app is not at fault. The trap is the helper, not the call: R138 fixed `LogOneOffDoseTest`'s copy (retry `NoSuchElementException` as well), and six other files kept the old one. All six now retry it the same way (`QuickAddMedicineTest`, `TodayAsksAmountTest`, `TodayDeferredAlarmTest`, `TodayNoReminderTest`, `TodayTakeAllTest`, `UpcomingDoseSheetTest`); `QuickAddMedicineTest` had the same `.single()` inside its `eventually` twice. The eighth copy (`WidgetHideNamesRefreshTest`) polls a counter and cannot throw it. Unverified locally: the race needs the write to lose, which a local run did not reproduce; the failure is the CI log of run 41497508420. Test code only. | ✅ Resolved |  |
